Read this honestly. Fake money: every bet here is a simulation. Dollars add up books with different stake sizes, so one aggressive book can outweigh several careful ones — the units number evens that out. A day is the day the bet was placed (the slate the bot bet), not the day it was graded. Pushes and voids give the stake back and count as $0. Only graded bets appear; a bet placed today shows up after the nightly settle. Consensus re-bets picks the other bots already made, in its own bankroll, so the same game can count twice in the fleet total. Gameday is its 4-pick market card at a flat $100 a pick; its stats-model race card is a second opinion on the same games and is left out. Refused bets (the shadow logs) are never counted. Nine bets from two doubleheaders (07-17 and 07-28) were once dropped by the archive because both games had the same name and stake; they were recovered from the bots' saved history and are included. Most bets show what was bet after the game ("DET @ CAR · CAR +3.5", "Over 7.5", "CHW to win"). 551 older bets from books that have since been reset list the game only: the archive kept the game and the price but not the side.
